Over the last 24h the live journal shows 476 `POST /v2/activities/recommend` and ZERO 'successor-features blend applied' lines. ψ has entered the selection argmax zero times in 476 opportunities. ### `L3-edge-11` — composition-edge-reconcile → activity_composition_graph (per-edge success/execution counts)

- **Datum:** One row per (parent_activity_id, child_activity_id) with execution_count, success_count, weight, edge_kind, genuine.
- **Producer:** scripts/substrate/composition-edge-reconcile.ts:479-486 — `UPSERT activity_composition_graph:\`${ridOf(key)}\` CONTENT {parent_activity_id, child_activity_id, execution_count, success_count, weight, success, org_id:'organizations:substrate', account_id_version:0, execution_id:'composition-edge-reconcile', edge_kind, genuine, derived_from, …}`. CONDITION: run by the composition-edge-reconcile.timer; edges are derived from (1) composition_chain shape-flow — requires `intersects(a.output_impulse_shapes, b.input_impulse_shapes)` (:321) — and (2) task-level `consumed_from_task_ids` provenance (:341-357). Malformed pairs are skipped with a console.error (:464). Second, mostly-unused producer: repos/activity-api/src/routes/activities.ts:7885-7891 (`POST /v2/activities/composition`) — no in-repo caller found for that route.
- **Consumer:** repos/activity-api/src/services/discover-by-shapes.ts:168-176 — the correlated subquery attached only when `isCandidatesMode`; predecessor branch `WHERE parent_activity_id = $predecessor_activity_id AND child_activity_id = $parent.id`, else `GROUP ALL` branch `WHERE child_activity_id = $parent.id`. Also read by pattern-miner.ts:264, impulses.ts:2407, activities.ts:7729/:8027.
- **Value form:** ⚠ CONFIRMED live 2026-08-21 via `GET /v2/activities/composition/graph?limit=3`: `child_activity_id` / `parent_activity_id` are stored as STRINGS of the form `"activity:⟨ladderwalk:lw_5⟩"` — and also as `"satisfier:code_modification_proposal"` for satisfier pseudo-activities. Schema `TYPE string` (sql/002-learning-system-phase1.surql:21-25). The consumer compares that string to `$parent.id`, which is a SurrealDB RECORD LINK, not a string. Note also that the ψ table stores the SAME activity with the wrapper STRIPPED (normalizeActivityId) — three id value-forms in one learning loop.
- **Suspected:** AGREEMENT: SUSPECTED BROKEN, UNVERIFIED — if SurrealDB does not equate `string("activity:⟨x⟩")` with a record link, `comp_row` is null on BOTH branches and composition_score has never been non-null. Supporting live evidence: a read-only discover probe with include_scores returned `composition_score: null` on 5/5 candidates. NOT conclusive alone (an empty GROUP ALL also yields execution_count 0 → null). DISCRIMINATING PROBE: `POST /v2/activities/discover-by-shapes {include_scores:true, predecessor_activity_id:"activity:⟨ladderwalk:lw_4⟩", required_shapes:<lw_5's output_shapes>}` and check whether the known 3-execution lw_4→lw_5 edge surfaces a non-null composition_score. LIVENESS: composition-edge-reconcile.timer is `inactive` (masked) — 2,029 rows are historical. Instrument caveat: `/v2/activities/composition/graph` reports `"total":0` while returning edges — verified again today.
- **VERDICT — agreement `FAIL` · liveness `UNOBSERVABLE-PLANE-MASKED` · visibility `NEGATIVE` · load-bearing `critical`**
  - agreement: CONFIRMED FAIL — the inventory's own #1 unverified item, now settled by live measurement. I read the subquery verbatim: discover-by-shapes.ts:168-176 uses raw `child_activity_id = $parent.id` with NO cast (`<string>`, `record::id()`, `type::thing()`), and the outer SELECT is `FROM activity` (:233-239) so `$parent.id` is a SurrealDB RECORD LINK, while activity_composition_graph.child_activity_id is TYPE string (sql/002-learning-system-phase1.surql:21-25) holding values like `"activity:⟨ladderwalk:lw_5⟩"` and `"satisfier:code_modification_proposal"` (confirmed live). THE DISCRIMINATING MEASUREMENT: I fetched all 2,029 edges, extracted the 385 distinct children, and cross-referenced them against live discover-by-shapes candidate lists. 35 candidates ARE known edge children with substantial evidence — activity:⟨auto-bridge-code_quality⟩ (320 executions), ⟨auto-bridge-problem_detection⟩ (242), ⟨auto-bridge-source_code⟩ (204), ⟨compose-analyze-source-to-concept-to-development-vessel:apply-proposal-as-patch⟩ (88) — and EVERY ONE returned composition_score: null. The subquery matches nothing even where the edge demonstrably exists. Two mechanisms remain consistent with this (string-vs-record-link inequality, or `$parent.id` failing to bind inside the correlated subquery); both are covered by the same fix. Note the textual forms DO match — candidate activity_id is also `activity:⟨…⟩` — so this is a TYPE mismatch, not a naming one, and no amount of string-level inspection would have found it.
  - liveness: The WRITE side is masked: composition-edge-reconcile.timer is inactive, so the 2,029 rows are historical, not flow. The READ side I measured directly and it is structurally zero regardless (see agreement). PROBE for the write side once unmasked: db_admin diagnose on activity_composition_graph before/after a timer run, expecting row_counts.activity_composition_graph to rise. PROBE for the read side after the cast fix: re-run POST /v2/activities/discover-by-shapes {"required_shapes":["code_quality"],"include_scores":true,"mode":"forward","limit":300} and assert activity:⟨auto-bridge-code_quality⟩ returns composition_score with sample_count 320, not null.
  - visibility: Two concealing layers, both verified today. (a) A null composition_score is INDISTINGUISHABLE from 'this candidate has no edges yet' — the producer at :328-341 emits null both when comp_row is absent and when execution_count is 0, so a total type-level failure looks exactly like a cold start, which is why this survived. (b) The dedicated instrument lies: GET /v2/activities/composition/graph?limit=3000 returns 2029 edges (exactly matching db_admin row_counts.activity_composition_graph = 2029, so the list itself is complete at that limit — the brief's '~19% unreachable' did not reproduce) but reports `"total": 0`. Anyone checking graph health via that field sees an empty graph.
  - **what must happen:** In repos/activity-api/src/services/discover-by-shapes.ts:172 and :175 compare like with like: `child_activity_id = <string>$parent.id` (and confirm the correlated binding resolves) — or, better, normalise the whole learning loop onto ONE id value-form, because activity_composition_graph stores `activity:⟨x⟩`, successor_features stores bare `x` via normalizeActivityId, and /recommend's relevancy path uses `activity:⟨x⟩` via normalizeRecordId. Three forms for one activity is the root cause of both this seam and L3-relevance-02. Also fix the `total` field on GET /v2/activities/composition/graph so the instrument stops reporting an empty graph. Detector: an integration assertion that for a known edge with execution_count > 0, the discover response returns a non-null composition_score — the same query I ran today, run automatically.

### `L3-edge-12` — composition_score → blendEdgeScore → WalkCandidate.sampledScore (per-edge evidence entering the pick)

- **Datum:** `composition_score: {alpha, beta, sample_count, predecessor_id}` per candidate, blended into the walk's selection score.
- **Producer:** repos/activity-api/src/services/discover-by-shapes.ts:328-341 — `composition_score = compRow && (compRow.execution_count||0) > 0 ? {alpha: success_count+1, beta: (execution_count−success_count)+1, sample_count: execution_count, predecessor_id} : null`. CONDITION: `isCandidatesMode` (mode === 'candidates_with_scores' OR include_scores === true, :167) AND a non-null comp_row with execution_count > 0.
- **Consumer:** repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:6027-6035 — `const edge = x.composition_score as EdgeEvidence` → `blendEdgeScore(globalScore, edge, betaSample(edgeAlpha, edgeBeta), k)` → `sampledScore` (:6041). Weighting in repos/goal-host-vessel/src/edge-blend.ts:67-73: `w = samples/(samples+k)`, EDGE_BLEND_K = 10 (:50), and `edgeWeight` returns 0 — leaving the score EXACTLY the global draw — for absent, malformed, or zero-sample evidence (:69-70). Downstream: producer-pick.ts:34 grants rank −1 when `sampledScore > 0.5`.
- **Value form:** Producer emits an OBJECT; the consumer reads it as an object (edge-blend.ts:52-56 EdgeEvidence) — this half was REPAIRED (index.ts:6019-6021 carries the fix comment: it previously sat in a `numOr(...)` chain and always yielded undefined). `alpha`/`beta` are `success_count+1` / `failures+1` ints; `k` is threaded from selection-tuning.ts with the compiled-in 10 as fallback (:6004).
- **Suspected:** AGREEMENT: healed at this hop. But the hop is starved upstream by L3-edge-11 — live probe returned composition_score null on 5/5 candidates, so `edgeWeight` is 0 and `blendEdgeScore` returns the global draw unchanged. The blend COMPUTES but does not ORDER. LIVENESS: UNOBSERVABLE-PLANE-MASKED (goal-host inactive). PROBE: a second dispatch over the same first activity, then assert `composition_score.sample_count > 0` on the discover response for the picked candidate — assert the ordering INPUT arrived, not a rank inversion (at K=10 one edge success weights ≈0.09).
- **VERDICT — agreement `PASS` · liveness `UNOBSERVABLE-PLANE-MASKED` · visibility `NONE` · load-bearing `high`**
  - agreement: Healed at this hop, exactly as the inventory says, and I confirmed the repair verbatim. goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:6027-6035 reads `x.composition_score` as an EdgeEvidence OBJECT and passes it to blendEdgeScore with a betaSample over edge.alpha/edge.beta; the comment at :6019-6021 records that it previously sat in the `numOr(...)` chain and therefore always yielded undefined. edge-blend.ts:67-73 `w = samples/(samples+k)` with EDGE_BLEND_K = 10, and edgeWeight returns 0 for absent/malformed/zero-sample evidence, leaving the score exactly the global draw. `k` is threaded from selection-tuning.ts with the compiled-in default, so an unthreaded call site is inert rather than mis-tuned. Producer types at discover-by-shapes.ts:328-341 match.
  - liveness: goal-host-vessel is inactive so the blend is not executing at all. Independently, the hop is starved upstream: I measured composition_score null on 293/293 live discover candidates including 35 with known edges (L3-edge-11), so even when goal-host runs, edgeWeight is 0 and blendEdgeScore returns the global draw unchanged — the blend COMPUTES but does not ORDER. PROBE once unmasked AND after the edge-11 cast fix: run a second dispatch over the same first activity, then assert `composition_score.sample_count > 0` on the discover response for the picked candidate. Assert the ordering INPUT arrived — do NOT assert a rank inversion, since at K=10 a single edge success weights only ≈0.09.
  - visibility: blendEdgeScore returns the unmodified global draw when evidence is missing — a legitimate, silent, correct-looking degradation. Nothing logs that per-edge evidence was absent, nothing counts how many candidates arrived with usable evidence, and the resulting sampledScore is a perfectly plausible number. The one place a signal could live cheaply — producer-pick.ts:34, which grants rank −1 when sampledScore > 0.5 — records no provenance for why the score was what it was.
  - **what must happen:** Nothing on the agreement here — fix L3-edge-11 and evidence starts arriving. What must be ADDED is the counter that makes starvation legible: in readCandidateShapes (repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:6027) tally how many candidates in a batch carried usable edge evidence and log it once per discover call (e.g. `edge_evidence: 3/50`). A run where that ratio is 0 across every step is the detector for an upstream break, and it is the number that would have caught edge-11 months ago. Do not change EDGE_BLEND_K until evidence actually flows — the right K is a measurement, not a guess.

Producer sends NO grounding fields at all, so `gvlGrounded` evaluates false on every oracle-mirrored label: the corpus's own quality metric is structurally pinned at 0 for the machine tier. `verdict` is the string 'achieved'/'not_achieved'; `notes` and `goal` are sliced to 600 chars at the producer.

The consumer derives gvlGrounded server-side at impulses.ts:2988-2993, requiring source ∈ [git, filesystem, process, http, journal, human] AND non-empty probe AND expected AND observed. None are sent, so `grounded` is structurally false for every machine-tier label — the corpus's own quality metric is pinned at 0 for the tier that produces almost all of its rows. ### `L3-gvl-14` — goal_verification_labels → read back, but by nothing that selects

- **Datum:** The most recent label for one execution_id: {verdict, notes, labeler}.
- **Producer:** repos/activity-api/src/routes/impulses.ts:2876 (and the dead duplicate at :3078) — `SELECT * FROM goal_verification_labels ${whereClause} ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $limit`, returned as `content: JSON.stringify(rows)`.
- **Consumer:** repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:14847-14905 `maybeConsumeOracleLabel(record)` — resolves `{type:'goal_verification_label', execution_id, limit:1}`, parses `labelPayload.content`, and on `label.labeler === 'human'` sets `record.reached`, `record.humanGraded`, `record.humanReachNotes` and prefixes `record.goalReachReason` (:14894-14899). CONDITION: `record.status !== 'running' && learn && !learn.oracleLabelWritten && record.executionId` (:14858); the latch is burned ONLY by a human label (:14893) — a deliberate fix, because recordDeterministicLabel always lands a machine row first and used to make the human branch unreachable (0 human overrides in 48 h vs 81 machine consumptions). Second consumer: repos/obsidian-vessel/src/sync/improvement-sync.ts:128 (display only).
- **Value form:** Producer `content` is a JSON-stringified ARRAY; consumer parses and takes `labels[0]` — agrees, and relies on the producer's `ORDER BY created_at DESC` so a later human row outranks the earlier machine row at limit:1. `verdict` string compared against the exact literals 'achieved'|'not_achieved'|'partial' (:14877).
- **Suspected:** READ, BUT NOT BY ANYTHING THAT SELECTS. The consumer's own comment (:14856-14858) states reach is NEVER posterior-written from a label — so the oracle corpus, the destination of every provide_feedback verdict and every deterministic oracle judgement, terminates in an in-memory DispatchRecord field and a UI string. No path from the corpus to Thompson α/β, to successor_features, to the composition graph, or to any tuning param. This is the closing arc of the stretch and it is open: 18,005 labels, zero of them reach selection. VISIBILITY: unusually GOOD on this one hop — every non-consumption prints a `[oracle-label] NOT consumed exec=… reason=…` line with a discriminating reason (:14871, :14880). LIVENESS: UNOBSERVABLE-PLANE-MASKED (goal-host inactive). Probe: `journalctl -u goal-host-vessel | grep -c 'oracle-label'` split by `NOT consumed` vs `HUMAN reach override`.
- **VERDICT — agreement `PASS` · liveness `UNOBSERVABLE-PLANE-MASKED` · visibility `PASS` · load-bearing `critical`**
  - agreement: Both halves agree and I read the consumer verbatim. Producer impulses.ts:2876 returns `content: JSON.stringify(rows)` from `SELECT * FROM goal_verification_labels ... ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $limit`; consumer goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:14861-14877 parses `labelPayload.content` as an array and takes labels[0], comparing verdict against the exact literals 'achieved'|'not_achieved'|'partial'. The source-aware latch at :14893 is confirmed and is a genuinely good fix — the comment at :14884-14892 records that burning the latch on machine labels made the human branch structurally unreachable (0 human overrides in 48h vs 81 machine consumptions), and the ORDER BY DESC means a later human row is already labels[0] on the next poll. The DEFECT is not agreement: it is that the terminus is `record.reached`, `record.humanGraded`, `record.humanReachNotes` and a `goalReachReason` string prefix — in-memory DispatchRecord fields and a UI string. The comment at :14854-14856 states outright that reach is NEVER posterior-written from a label. So 18,005 labels — every provide_feedback verdict and every deterministic oracle judgement — reach nothing that selects: no Thompson α/β, no successor_features, no composition graph, no tuning param. This is the closing arc of the stretch and it is open.
  - liveness: goal-host-vessel is inactive so maybeConsumeOracleLabel is not running. PROBE once unmasked: `journalctl -u goal-host-vessel | grep -c 'oracle-label'` split by outcome — `NOT consumed` (with its discriminating reason field), `consumed automated verdict=`, and `HUMAN reach override`. The historical baseline recorded in the code comment is 81 machine consumptions to 0 human overrides in 48h, which is the number to compare against after the latch fix has had traffic. Note that even a rising consumption count would NOT be liveness for the learning loop — it only proves the label reached an in-memory field.
  - visibility: Unusually good on this one hop, and worth saying so: every non-consumption prints `[oracle-label] NOT consumed exec=… reason=…` with a DISCRIMINATING reason (fetch_not_ok+status at :14869, or no_labels | verdict_field_missing | verdict_unrecognised at :14879-14880, plus n_labels and labeler), and both success paths print at console.log with the verdict and the resulting record.reached. These are counted-at-the-consumer signals in everything but name. This is the model the rest of the stretch should copy. It does not, however, make the DEAD END visible — nothing reports that the consumed verdict influenced no selection.
  - **what must happen:** MEASURE FIRST, then wire — and the measurement is specific, not a hedge. Before connecting the corpus to selection, count how many of the 18,005 labels DISAGREE with the trace's own success flag for the same execution_id: `SELECT count() FROM goal_verification_labels` joined against execution traces, split by (verdict='achieved' vs trace.success). If the disagreement rate is near zero the corpus carries no information the posterior lacks and the honest answer is to leave it as an audit record and say so. If it is material, the corpus is the highest-value ungraded signal in the system and needs a consumer that selects: the safe first one is not a β-penalty (the comment at :14854-14856 correctly warns that re-writing β would double-count and could condemn a correctly-selected arm on an unreachable goal, law 12) but a HUMAN-labelled counterfactual set used to calibrate the reach judge itself. Detector either way: a scheduled report of label-vs-trace disagreement rate, which is also the metric that tells you whether the reach gate is drifting.

**Stretch summary.** Validated all 14 seams against the three-condition rubric. The inventory is substantially accurate; I upgraded three of its own UNVERIFIED/suspected items to CONFIRMED by observation and corrected four claims. CONFIRMED FAILS newly settled: (a) L3-relevance-02 — the store holds BARE activity ids ("auto-bridge-author_producer", live INFO line Aug 16 23:53:23) while the consumer queries with PREFIXED ids (normalizeRecordId is a passthrough, surrealdb-types.ts:52-66, and recommend candidates carry no `t.id`, only variant_id='activity:⟨…⟩' — verified live), so `IN $activity_ids` is structurally empty; (b) L3-edge-11 — 35 discover candidates that ARE known composition-graph children (exec_count up to 320) ALL returned composition_score:null, so the correlated subquery matches nothing; (c) L3-tuning-06 — the exact SurrealDB error "Found NULL for field `evidence` … but expected a option<string>" is in the live journal with params {updated_by:null, evidence:null}. Two liveness verdicts are FAIL by observation rather than plane-masked, because activity-api is up and logs at INFO: 476 POST /recommend in 24h with ZERO "successor-features blend applied" lines (L3-psi-10), and ZERO POST /v2/activities/impulse-relevance in 24h (L3-relevance-01). CORRECTIONS to the inventory: the tuning NULL rejection is raised on the UPDATE branch against an existing record, not silently on create — and the create branch is NOT uniformly silent (PROBE_D landed, PROBE_E with identical meta did not), so the branch mechanism is an open measurement, though the fix is robust to it; impulses.ts has EIGHT `case 'goal_verification_label'` labels, not two, seven unreachable; the composition/graph instrument returned the COMPLETE 2029 edges (matching db_admin row_count) at limit=3000 so the brief's "~19% unreachable" did not reproduce, but `total:0` is confirmed; and the inventory's note 7 claim of "no writes" is contradicted by PROBE_* POST /v2/tuning-params writes at 06:42-06:43 from a separate session (I made none). Method note: LOG_LEVEL defaults to info (config.ts:237) and 0 DEBUG lines appear in 24h, so every probe that greps a logger.debug line measures the FILTER, not the seam — that invalidates the inventory's suggested probe for L3-relevance-02 and L3-sf-write-04. # Addendum — the two previously-blocked stretches (2026-08-21, run 2)

Enumerated and validated with no production access. These are the stretches that
follow the impulse BODY (R3) and the credit KEY (L1) — the two the first run lost.


## RECALL 3 — dispatch, binding, and content threading into the trace (goal-host walk → ias-executor engine → trace sink → activity-api ingest → reach verdict write-back)


> METHOD + ACCESS: no production access used. All claims are from reading HEAD in this repo plus `git log -L` blame and one local test run (`bun test repos/activity-api/src/routes/trace-key-agreement.test.ts` → 4 pass). No credentials read, no ssh, no docker exec. Every liveness verdict is UNOBSERVABLE-PENDING-LOCAL-STACK with an exact probe embedded in each seam's suspected_status; none of them is a liveness claim about the hub.

CROSS-REFERENCES to SEAM_INVENTORY_2026-08-21.md (not re-enumerated here): X-boundary-01 owns the flush → durable spool → drain hop that follows R3-trace-09 (the 4xx-deletes-the-trace-in-60s path); R3-trace-09 is scoped strictly to the PROJECTION (what is dropped on the way to the wire). L2-structure-01 (parent_execution_id across the vessel boundary) sits downstream of R3-execute-08. L2-structure-06 (composition-edge-reconcile, masked on the hub) is the consumer in R3-execute-06 and R3-trace-12. L2-structure-08/10/11 (reach → mintReachedTrace → ribosome extract → activityTemplate_write) consume the composite built in R3-trace-13. R1-store-14 (recordGoalPath / two hash identities) is the sibling of R3-reach-15's id-form problem. R2-select-07 (transformToLegacyTemplate) is the selection-side analogue of the key-by-key projections catalogued here. L3-gvl-13 consumes the verdict delivered in R3-reach-15.

THE FIVE LOUDEST NEGATIVE-VISIBILITY FINDINGS, restated so they are not buried:
1. repos/activity-api/src/routes/trace-key-agreement.test.ts:67 declares `consumed_from_task_ids` a KNOWN_ORPHAN and its third test ASSERTS the field has no reader — while scripts/substrate/composition-edge-reconcile.ts:342 reads it. The test's corpus scan (:79-83) covers only activity-api's own tree, so it is blind to scripts/. It passes today, it would keep passing after the reader was wired, and right now it actively misreports a live seam as dead. That is a detector whose worst case it cannot observe.
2. The hollow-walklog cap (index.ts:3375-3385) cannot fire on any walk that early-reaches, because the only call site that can trigger the early reach (:9471) omits walkEvidence and the short-circuit at :9563 then skips both call sites that carry it. The gate exists, is correct, and is unreachable on the population it was written for.
3. A stub pool entry (`{producedBy, executionId}`) is logged, counted and graded exactly like real content — index.ts:9422 emits `new_shapes=1` either way, and the reach digest renders both as `- <shape>: <json>`. The pool advances; the data does not.
4. execution_trace_content is written fire-and-forget (execution-traces.ts:2331) after the HTTP 200 has already been decided. If that insert stopped, every trace POST would still answer 200 and the composition graph would simply stop growing, with one uncounted logger.warn per loss.
5. Per-task `error` has no landing key in normalizePersistedTask, and failure_mode is flattened to a bare `{type:'execution_error'}` at the wire (trace-sink.ts:188-190). A failed execution therefore stores as a well-formed failure row with its diagnosis removed at two independent points.

AGREEMENT FAILURES SETTLED STATICALLY (no live access required to confirm any of these):
- engine.ts:1104 `accumulatedVariables[task.id] = (this as any).executionId` — ActivityExecutor (declared :141-142) has no such field, so the value is `undefined`. Blame: `git log -L 1104,1104:src/engine.ts` → b3b8411, Sat Aug 1 2026, 'substrate-authored: apply route-edit-ae36b57a:1-compose-report via mitosis cutover'; it replaced `cappedFirstText`. Downstream, VERIFIED both legs: in an LLM prompt, llm-prompt.ts:57 `if (cur === undefined || cur === null) return match` leaves the LITERAL `{{taskId}}` in the prompt; in a conditional gate, engine.interpolation.ts:77 → :202 throws UNRESOLVABLE_GATE and the whole execution fails. The sibling producer at engine.ts:780 still writes the correct value — same key, two value-forms by dispatch path.
- llm-prompt.ts:48's placeholder regex is `[\w]+(?:\.[\w]+)*`, so any shape name containing a colon (`obsidian:write_note`, `concept:create`) is structurally unmatchable as `{{shape}}` even though poolVars publishes it.
- addToPool (index.ts:6204) is first-write-wins with no upgrade path, and the horizontal fan-out branch (:9007) has no real-content branch at all.
- buildCompositeTraceFromChain (index.ts:5693-5710) sets `resolverId` to a SHAPE NAME and omits resolvedConfig / consumedFromTaskIds / childActivityId entirely — which is the mechanical origin of the 98-of-98-argument-less-tasks measurement quoted in activity-api's own source at execution-traces.ts:227-236.
- `failure_mode: {type:'execution_error'}` is not a member of FailureModeSchema's discriminatedUnion (schemas.ts:1080-1122) yet execution-traces.ts:75 depends on receiving it. This only works because the route deliberately does not enforce the schema (documented at :1925-1930, to avoid 400-ing the flat-envelope light-dispatch poster).

INCIDENTAL, not a seam: repos/ias-executor-ts/src/engine.ts:295-296 contains two committed `console.error("[rIS-debug]"/"[rIS-debug2]")` calls inside resolveImpulseSlot that dump impulse content previews (400 chars) to stderr on every slot resolution. Committed debug instrumentation on a hot path, writing impulse bodies to the journal.

UNVERIFIED: whether any shipped template actually uses a bare `{{<priorTaskId>}}` placeholder (the blast radius of the engine.ts:1104 regression) — I did not enumerate the template corpus. The grep to settle it is in that seam's liveness probe.


### `R3-execute-01` — Walk pool → host.runTemplate → ActivityExecutor.execute: the seeded impulse array and the decision-time inputShapes

- **Datum:** poolImpulses: Impulse[] (id `walk-<shape>-<n>`, pointer {type:'memo'}, metadata{shape,summary,producedBy:'goal-host-walk',goalSignature}, loaded:true, content) plus parentExecutionId + compositionChain
- **Producer:** repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:9341-9349 (single-pick step) and :8966-8974 (horizontal fan-out branch). CONDITION: fires once per walk step after a template is fetched (:9328) and never on the satisfier path, which builds a synthetic trace instead (:9271-9291). Passes `impulses: poolImpulses` — the SAME array object each step, so every step re-seeds the whole accumulated pool, not a delta.
- **Consumer:** repos/ias-executor-ts/src/hosts/goal-host.ts:897-903 runTemplate → executor.execute; repos/ias-executor-ts/src/engine.ts:150-153 `for (const impulse of seededImpulses) this.runtime.store.put(impulse)` and :176 `inputShapes = [...new Set(seededImpulses.map(getImpulseShape))]`. UNCONDITIONAL.
- **Value form:** WRITE: full Impulse objects with live `content` (any JS value). READ: store.put keyed by imp.id; only `metadata.shape` survives into trace.inputShapes (string[]). The CONTENT crosses the in-process boundary intact here — this is the last hop where it does.
- **VERDICT — agreement `PASS` · liveness `UNOBSERVABLE-PENDING-LOCAL-STACK` · visibility `WEAK` · load-bearing `high`**
  - agreement: CONFIRMED as filed. Producer index.ts:9341-9349 passes `impulses: poolImpulses`; consumer engine.ts:150-153 puts each into the store and :176 derives `inputShapes = [...new Set(seededImpulses.map(getImpulseShape))]`. Shape key is `metadata.shape` on both sides — mkImpulse stamps it at index.ts:6192, getImpulseShape reads it. The same array object is re-passed each step so the whole accumulated pool re-seeds (idempotent overwrite by `walk-<shape>-<n>` id). The engine comment at :173-175 states the intent explicitly: keying the state-conditioned Thompson posterior by the same shapes recommend reads. No key, type or value-form disagreement.
  - **probe:** Boot a local stack (`make -C scripts/substrate up ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=...`), dispatch one multi-step goal via `mcp__metabob__run_goal_async`, then query the local trace store: `SELECT execution_id, input_impulse_shapes, composition_chain FROM execution WHERE variant_id !~ '^composition:' ORDER BY executed_at DESC LIMIT 20;`. HEALTHY = 100% of walk-step rows have a non-empty input_impulse_shapes, and array::len grows monotonically with array::len(composition_chain). A row with input_impulse_shapes = NONE means the seam produced nothing on that hop.
  - visibility: The only signal is the walk's own tap line at index.ts:9422 (`step N ran ... pool=<n>`) — console-only, uncounted, and it reports pool SIZE not seeded-shape count. Nothing at the consuming side (engine.ts:176) counts how many shapes arrived. If the pool arrived empty, the log would still print a step.
  - **what must happen:** Agreement is healthy; the gap is visibility. Add a counter at the CONSUMING side — engine.ts:176 — emitting seeded-impulse count and distinct-shape count onto the trace (a `seeded_shape_count` field the sink already has a slot pattern for), and assert in activity-api ingest that a trace with composition_chain length > 0 carries input_impulse_shapes. That converts the existing WEAK log into a PASS counter readable by the same query that grades the walk.

### `R3-execute-02` — poolVars(): shape-name → variable binding (the ONLY channel that carries step N's content into step N+1's placeholders)

- **Datum:** Record<string,unknown> mapping each pool shape name to that impulse's raw `content`, plus `goal` (the goal TEXT, not the goal impulse object) and opts.variables
- **Producer:** repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:6212-6223 poolVars(). CONDITION: `v = { goal, ...opts.variables }` FIRST, then `for (const imp of poolImpulses) if (sh && !(sh in v)) v[sh] = imp.content` — caller variables and the literal key `goal` WIN over produced content, and the FIRST impulse of a shape wins over later ones. Called at :9341 and :8966 immediately before each dispatch.
- **Consumer:** repos/ias-executor-ts/src/engine.ts:198 `accumulatedVariables = {...options.variables}`, then repos/ias-executor-ts/src/resolvers/llm-prompt.ts:47-66 interpolate() for prompts and engine.interpolation.ts:201-202 resolveDottedPath for conditional gates. CONDITION for gates: a missing key THROWS UNRESOLVABLE_GATE and fails the whole execution (interpolation.ts:172,202).
- **Value form:** WRITE: `{ <shapeName>: <raw content, any type> }` — object contents are NOT stringified. READ (prompt): llm-prompt.ts:57-62 stringifies numbers/booleans, JSON.stringifies objects, and returns the LITERAL `{{x}}` when the value is undefined/null. READ (gate): presence-only; undefined ⇒ throw. A shape name containing `:` (e.g. `obsidian:write_note`) can NEVER be interpolated — llm-prompt.ts's regex is `[\w]+(?:\.[\w]+)*`, so colon-bearing shapes are silently unreachable as `{{shape}}`.
- **VERDICT — agreement `FAIL` · liveness `UNOBSERVABLE-PENDING-LOCAL-STACK` · visibility `NONE` · load-bearing `critical`**
  - agreement: CONFIRMED, and STRONGER than filed. poolVars (index.ts:6212-6223) is exactly as described: `{ goal, ...opts.variables }` first, then `if (sh && !(sh in v)) v[sh] = imp.content` — caller vars and the literal key `goal` win, first impulse of a shape wins. Consumer regex confirmed at llm-prompt.ts:48: `/\{\{([\w]+(?:\.[\w]+)*)\}\}/g` — a colon cannot match. CORRECTION/SHARPENING: (a) the inventory understates the reach — a THIRD interpolator it never names, goal-host's own interpolateProxyValue at index.ts:12927, carries the SAME `[\w]+(?:\.[\w]+)*` alternative (it special-cased only the `impulse:` prefix), so the colon-shape drop applies on the proxy path too; (b) the claim is LIVE, not merely structural — colon-bearing shapes are real pool members: `obsidian:write_note` is resolved and pooled at index.ts:10060 and the terminal-rewrite logic at :6819 turns `obsidian:note` into `obsidian:write_note`. Value-form asymmetry confirmed: llm-prompt.ts:57 `if (cur === undefined || cur === null) return match` (literal passthrough) vs engine.interpolation.ts:77 → :202 `if (!res.found) throw unresolvable(...)` (fatal).
  - **probe:** On a local stack dispatch a goal whose walk produces a colon-bearing shape (e.g. "write a note to Obsidian summarising X" → obsidian:write_note) plus one multi-step goal whose step-2 prompt binds `{{<shape produced by step 1>}}`. Then: `SELECT execution_id, tasks FROM execution_trace_content ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 20;` and grep every `tasks[*].resolved_config` for a surviving literal `{{`. HEALTHY = ZERO `{{` substrings across 20 consecutive multi-step executions. Any surviving `{{obsidian:...}}` is the colon drop; any surviving `{{<bareShape>}}` is a pool-binding miss.
  - visibility: An un-interpolated `{{x}}` reaching an LLM produces a fluent, plausible answer and no error, no warning, no counter — on all three interpolators. The gate leg throws, but only for the small population of tasks that use conditional gates.
  - **what must happen:** Two changes, both cheap. (1) Widen the placeholder token grammar to `[\w:]+(?:\.[\w]+)*` in ALL THREE interpolators — llm-prompt.ts:48, engine.interpolation.ts's tokenizer, and goal-host index.ts:12927 — with a shared exported regex constant so they cannot drift again (they already have, three times). (2) Make the silent leg loud: in llm-prompt.ts:57 and index.ts:12940, when a placeholder resolves to undefined, push the token onto a per-task `unresolvedPlaceholders: string[]` on the task record, ship it through the sink, and give it a landing key in normalizePersistedTask. Then the counter is `SELECT count() FROM execution_trace_content WHERE tasks[WHERE array::len(unresolved_placeholders) > 0]` — healthy = 0. That is the detector; today there is none.

### `R3-execute-03` — Trace outputs → addToPool: first-write-wins, and the stub-vs-real-content fork

- **Datum:** (shape, content) pair entering producedShapes + poolImpulses
- **Producer:** THREE writers with different content forms. (a) repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:9386 `addToPool(shape, imp.content, ...)` — REAL content, CONDITION: task.success !== false AND host.runtime.store.get(id) returns an impulse AND imp.metadata.shape is set AND shape !== 'activityExecutionSummary' AND content not null/undefined. (b) :9392 `addToPool(s, {producedBy, executionId}, '(stub)')` — METADATA STUB, condition: declared outputShapes not already in the pool. (c) :9007 horizontal fan-out — STUB ONLY, there is no real-content branch on that path at all.
- **Consumer:** repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:6196-6207 addToPool — `if (!shape || producedShapes.has(shape)) return;` FIRST-WRITE-WINS. Downstream readers: poolVars (R3-execute-02), poolDigest at :9520-9528 and interimPool at :9456-9464 (the reach-gate's evidence), poolProvenance mirror at :8319.
- **Value form:** WRITE (a): the resolver's genuine payload. WRITE (b)/(c): `{producedBy: '<templateId>', executionId: '<exec_...>'}` — a two-field metadata object with ZERO of the produced data. READ: both are indistinguishable to poolVars and to the digest builder, which renders them identically as `- <shape>: <JSON>`.
- **VERDICT — agreement `FAIL` · liveness `UNOBSERVABLE-PENDING-LOCAL-STACK` · visibility `NEGATIVE` · load-bearing `critical`**
  - agreement: CONFIRMED exactly as filed. addToPool at index.ts:6197-6206 opens with `if (!shape || producedShapes.has(shape)) return;` — first-write-wins, no upgrade path. Within a single step the real-content branch (:9375-9387) does run BEFORE the stub branch (:9390-9394) for the same task, so a task that recovers content wins over its own declared-shape stub; but ACROSS tasks and ACROSS steps a stub is permanent. The horizontal fan-out branch is stub-only as claimed — I read index.ts:8981-9007 in full and there is no store.get / real-content branch anywhere in it, only `addToPool(s, { producedBy: c.id, executionId: t.id }, ...)` at :9007. Both writers render identically to the consumer: poolVars (:6221 `v[sh] = imp.content`) and the digest builders (interimPool :9456-9464, poolDigest :9520-9528) emit `- <shape>: <JSON>` with no discriminator.
  - **probe:** On a local stack, after any finished dispatch: `mcp__metabob__resolve_impulse({shape:'goalWalkState', pointer:{dispatchId:'<id>'}})` and read `poolProvenance[*].contentPreview` / `.chars` (built at index.ts:8319-8338). HEALTHY = at most ~1 entry per walk whose contentPreview matches /"producedBy"/ with chars in the 40-90 band. A walk where the MAJORITY of entries match that pattern is threading metadata, not data. Run 20 dispatches; healthy = stub fraction < 0.2. Second probe for the fan-out specifically: dispatch a goal that triggers the OR-edge bundle and assert its poolProvenance is 100% stubs (expected FAIL by construction, and that number is the size of the defect).
  - visibility: LOUD. index.ts:9422 emits `new_shapes=1` for a stub exactly as for real content, the walk's progress logic (`progressed = producedShapes.size > beforeSize`) counts a stub as progress, ledgerStep credits it, and the reach digest hands the stub to the LLM judge rendered as content. Every observable says the walk advanced. poolProvenance renders the stub honestly in the Obsidian panel but has no counter, no threshold and no alert — a human would have to look.
  - **what must happen:** Three concrete changes. (1) FILE index.ts, addToPool: add a `stub: boolean` parameter and let a real-content write REPLACE a stub for the same shape (relax :6204 to `if (!shape) return; if (producedShapes.has(shape) && !existingIsStub) return;`). Stubs must be upgradable or they must not enter the pool. (2) KEY: stamp `metadata.stub = true` on stub impulses in mkImpulse so poolVars, the digest builders and the reach judge can tell them apart — and EXCLUDE stubs from the reach contentDigest entirely, since a `{producedBy,executionId}` object is not evidence of anything. (3) COUNTER/DETECTOR: change the tap at :9422 to `new_shapes=N (real=R stub=S)` and add a walk-level `stub_shape_fraction` onto the trace; alert when it exceeds 0.5. Give the horizontal fan-out at :9007 the same store.get real-content branch the single-pick path has at :9375-9387 — today it structurally cannot produce real content.

### `R3-execute-04` — Impulse-store lifetime: the engine evicts before the walk reads it back (top-level clear), and the reachContentDigests side-channel that exists to compensate

- **Datum:** Output-impulse CONTENT, addressed by impulse id after the child execution returned
- **Producer:** repos/ias-executor-ts/src/engine.ts:1337-1355 emits `lifecycle:execution:succeeded` carrying {executionId, outputImpulseIds} WHILE the store is live, then :1356-1360 evictExecutionScope with `isTopLevel: !options.parentExecutionId && compositionChain.length === 0` → :1484-1487 `store.impulses.clear()` (EVERYTHING) — CONDITION: only the SUCCESS branch emits the lifecycle event; the failure branch at :1434-1444 evicts with no emit.
- **Consumer:** TWO. (a) repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:9375-9387 reads `host.runtime.store.get(id)` AFTER runTemplate returned — for the first engine-run step of a walk (lastExecId === opts.parentExecutionId === undefined and chainExecIds empty, index.ts:8257,8260) the store was just cleared, so every get() misses and the walk falls through to the stub branch at :9390. (b) repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:12555-12582 captureReachDigest, wired via CapturingEventSink at :12584-12589 — snapshots content at emit time, keyed by executionId, LRU-capped at 100 (:12554,12577-12580).
- **Value form:** WRITE (store): live Impulse objects. READ (a): `undefined` for the first engine step; real impulses for nested steps. READ (b): a flattened string `- <shape>: <content sliced to 600>` joined and sliced to 4000 chars, keyed by `exec_<id>`.
- **VERDICT — agreement `FAIL` · liveness `UNOBSERVABLE-PENDING-LOCAL-STACK` · visibility `NEGATIVE` · load-bearing `critical`**
  - agreement: CONFIRMED, conditionally, exactly as filed — and the defect is DOCUMENTED BY THE AUTHOR. engine.ts:1356-1360 evicts with `isTopLevel: !options.parentExecutionId && compositionChain.length === 0`; :1484-1487 clears the ENTIRE store on that branch, :1488-1494 evicts only seeded inputs otherwise. For the first walk step, lastExecId is undefined and chainExecIds is empty (index.ts:8257, 9345-9346), so isTopLevel is TRUE and the walk's store.get at :9377 misses for every output — falling through to the stub branch. Nested steps survive. The success-only claim holds: the lifecycle emit is at :1337-1355 inside the success path; the failure branch (:1425-1444) evicts with no emit. captureReachDigest confirmed at index.ts:12554-12582 (LRU cap 100 at :12554, eviction at :12577-12580), and its own header comment at :12539-12552 states the problem verbatim: "runGoal/runTemplate returns (evictExecutionScope, isTopLevel), so the reach-gate can't read produced content post-hoc." NET-NEW: the fan-out makes this worse — index.ts:8960-8979 runs branches under Promise.all against ONE shared store, and each branch whose bundleParentExecId is undefined satisfies isTopLevel and calls store.impulses.clear(), so a completing branch wipes its still-running siblings' outputs mid-bundle. Race, not just loss.
  - **probe:** On a local stack, dispatch a 3-step goal. Probe A: `journalctl -u goal-host -n 500 | grep 'step . ran'` and cross-read the goalWalkState poolProvenance — HEALTHY = step 1's shapes carry a contentPreview that is NOT `{"producedBy":...}`; today expect step 1 = 100% stub, steps 2+ = real. That step-1-vs-step-N asymmetry IS the measurement. Probe B (digest coverage): count `lifecycle:execution:succeeded` emissions in the journal against walk-step count over 20 dispatches — HEALTHY = 1:1; every failed step contributes none, so any shortfall equals the ungraded-content population. Probe C (fan-out race): dispatch a bundle goal twice and diff the produced shape sets — HEALTHY = identical; nondeterministic shape sets across identical runs is the clear() race.
  - visibility: LOUD. The walk logs `new_shapes=1` for the stub the store-clear forced (index.ts:9422), goal_status reports the step as progress, and the reach gate is compensated by a side-channel (reachContentDigests) that repairs only the JUDGE's view and never the pool the next step binds from. So the reach verdict can be graded on content the next step provably did not receive. The pool advanced; the data did not; every instrument agrees it went fine.
  - **what must happen:** The correct fix is at the eviction boundary, not another side-channel. FILE engine.ts:1356-1360: stop letting the ENGINE decide isTopLevel from the absence of a parent id — a walk step is not a top-level execution, it is a member of a chain the walk owns. Add an explicit `retainOutputs?: boolean` to ExecuteOptions, set it from goal-host's runTemplate call (goal-host.ts:897-903) for every walk step, and have evictExecutionScope honour it: clear seeded inputs, retain outputs, and let the WALK release the scope when the walk ends. That single change fixes the step-1 stub, removes the need for the reachContentDigests LRU entirely, and eliminates the fan-out clear() race (no branch would clear). COUNTER: assert `store.impulses.size === 0` at walk teardown so a retained scope cannot leak. Until that lands, the fan-out race alone justifies serialising the bundle or passing a per-branch child store.

ActivityExecutor is declared at :141-142 with only `private readonly runtime`; I grepped every `this.executionId` / `executionId =` in the file — the only assignments are the LOCAL const at :145 and `accumulatedVariables.executionId` at :243-244. There is no instance field, so the value is `undefined`. Blame reproduced: `git log -L 1104,1104:src/engine.ts` → b3b8411 'substrate-authored: apply route-edit-ae36b57a:1-compose-report via mitosis cutover', replacing `cappedFirstText`. Sibling at :780 still writes `cappedFirst` correctly — same key, two value-forms by dispatch path. But that one live template runs on the dev-vessel CLI path — observe-orthogonal-refresh.ts:34-40 spawns `cli.ts run-activity`, and cli.ts:168 propagates `vars[task.id] = body` CORRECTLY. So there is NO demonstrated live outage; :1104 is irrelevant to that instance. Runtime half, on a local stack: (A) author a two-task probe template whose second task's prompt is exactly `{{first_task}}`, dispatch it through goal-host (engine path, NOT the CLI), then `SELECT tasks FROM execution_trace_content WHERE execution_id = <id>;` and read tasks[1].resolved_config — HEALTHY = no literal `{{first_task}}`; expected today = the literal survives. (B) `journalctl -u goal-host -u development-vessel --since '-1h' | grep -c UNRESOLVABLE_GATE` — HEALTHY = 0. Nothing anywhere counts unresolved placeholders.

FILE repos/ias-executor-ts/src/engine.ts:1104 — restore `accumulatedVariables[task.id] = cappedFirstText;` (cappedFirstText is already computed on the line above and is otherwise used only for `_text`). DETECTOR (the class, not the instance — law 6): a unit test in ias-executor-ts asserting that after every task with ≥1 stored output, `accumulatedVariables[task.id]` is defined and equals `accumulatedVariables[task.id + '_text']`, which pins the CLI/engine parity contract that engine.ts:1099-1103 and index.ts:12843 both assert in prose and nothing enforces. []) > 0 ORDER BY executed_at DESC LIMIT 50;`. (2) For every element of every returned chain: `SELECT count() FROM execution WHERE execution_id = <elem>;`. HEALTHY = 100% resolve to 1. Any `walk-satisfier-*` element resolving to 0 is a dangling chain link — and the count of those, divided by total chain elements, is the exact size of the reconcile's silent-skip population.
  - visibility: Nothing anywhere validates chain referential integrity — not at ingest (which trusts the client chain verbatim at :1868-1871), not at the reconcile (which `continue`s), not in any health check. A chain of entirely-dangling ids and a chain with no predecessors produce byte-identical downstream behaviour.
  - **what must happen:** Make the miss and the no-op different observables. (1) FILE scripts/substrate/composition-edge-reconcile.ts:316-318: split the `continue` into two counters — `predecessor_absent` (chain had no predecessor, benign) and `predecessor_unresolvable` (the id named a row that does not exist, a defect) — and emit both in the run's final JSON line. That single split converts NONE to PASS. (2) FILE repos/activity-api/src/routes/execution-traces.ts:1868-1877: on ingest of a non-empty client chain, count elements that do not resolve to an execution row into a `dangling_chain_links` metric. Do NOT reject — rejecting would drop traces — but stop trusting silently. The conditional write is confirmed at execution-traces.ts:1959-1963 — `...(body.input_impulse_shapes && body.input_impulse_shapes.length > 0 ? {...} : {})` — so an empty array writes NO COLUMN and is indistinguishable from a poster that never sent the field. The same `signature` column therefore holds two incompatible quantities — state-CONSUMED (tiers 1-2) and state-PRODUCED (tier 3) — and no reader can tell which tier answered, so a conditional Thompson cell can be keyed by an output signature and read by an input one.

  - visibility: deriveSignatureShapes returns a plain string[] with no provenance marker (:133-165), so no reader — not the Thompson updater, not coverage_tick, not any dashboard — can tell whether a given signature was derived from consumed state or produced state. The substitution is silent by construction, and the empty-array conditional write at :1959-1963 makes 'sent nothing' and 'sent empty' the same observable too.

(1) FILE repos/activity-api/src/routes/execution-traces.ts:133-165: change deriveSignatureShapes to return `{shapes, tier}` and persist `signature_derivation_tier` alongside `signature` on the execution row. Without that column the contamination is unmeasurable, so this is the 'measure first' step and it is one field. (2) Once measurable: if tier 3 is a non-trivial fraction, it must not write to the same `signature` column — an output-derived signature keyed into a cell that recommend reads as an input state is a wrong key, not a coarse one, and law 12 says record the intervention. Either write tier-3 signatures to a distinct column or refuse to derive a signature at all when no input pool was recorded. (3) FILE :1959-1963: write the column unconditionally (empty array included) so 'no input state' and 'field never sent' stop being the same observable. (4) Drop the dead `trace.output_impulses[*].shape` limb at :158-161 — this sink never populates it, so it is a fallback that cannot fire.

Fires when `(gapsFiled > 0 && producedShapes.length <= 2) || allStepsHollow`, where allStepsHollow requires ≥1 line matching `/\bstep \d+ ran /` and ALL such lines matching `/\bnew_shapes=0\b/`. Returns a deterministic `reached:false` and SKIPS the LLM judge.
- **Value form:** OMITTED (undefined) at THREE call sites: :9471 (the mid-walk interim check — 5 args), :4591 (the universal-tool-fallback floor — 5 args), :12304 (the single-template goal-reach path — 4 args). PASSED at :9564/:9570.

The cap is at :3374-3385, guarded `if (walkEvidence)` and fail-open by design (:3374 comment). The bypass is confirmed: the interim check at :9471 sits inside the progressed branch and on `interim.reached === true` sets earlyReachVerdict and BREAKS the walk (:9476-9481); the end-of-walk gate is `let verdict = earlyReachVerdict ?? await verifyGoalReached(..., walkEv)` (:9563-9564), so whenever the early check greens, NEITHER call site carrying walkEvidence ever executes. []) <= 2;` — HEALTHY ≈ 0; every such row is a reach the cap should have caught.

- **Datum:** {execution_id, reached: boolean, completion_shapes: string[]} — the graded outcome, and (at the consumer) the α/β credit that rides on it

The AET UPDATE at :4378-4382 is gated on isDualWriteEnabled() and the comment at :4360-4370 states it matched 0 rows on 143/143 successful patches. Only ids that name an actual `execution` row can match; the edit-intent and template-repair forms name git shas and variant ids, not executions.

### `R3-reach-16` — recordStep → WalkStep[] → in-process dispatch record only; POST /deliberation has no producer anywhere

- **Datum:** The per-step deliberation record: {index, at, selected{templateId,source,sampledScore,alpha,beta}, candidates[] with rejectedBecause, excluded[], status, newShapes[], rationale, poolBefore[], poolAfter[]}
- **Producer:** repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:8354-8357 recordStep, called at :8717, :9029, :9300, :9400, :10034, :10068, :10146. The ONLY durability is repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts:15517-15523 persistDispatchStore — `Bun.write(DISPATCH_STORE_PATH, JSON.stringify([...executionStore.values()]))` on a 5s interval, with pruneStore capping the store at 100 records and dropping the oldest 20 (:15524-15529).
- **Consumer:** INTENDED: repos/activity-api/src/routes/execution-traces.ts:4258-4299 `app.post('/deliberation')`, which appends the steps into the trace's FLEXIBLE `impulse_resolutions[]` on BOTH activity_execution_traces and `execution`, tagged `{deliberation:true, resolver_tier:'deliberation'}`. ACTUAL: no producer exists. `grep -rn deliberation repos/ scripts/ packages/ validation/scripts` over the whole super-repo returns only this route, three unrelated prose comments in obsidian-vessel and operator-goal-generator, and one test name. NOTHING POSTs to it.
- **Value form:** WRITE (goal-host): camelCase WalkStep objects in a JSON file at DISPATCH_STORE_PATH, plus the goalWalkState read shape. READ (route, if anything called it): `body.steps[]` capped at 60, each spread verbatim into impulse_resolutions. The two forms are compatible — the wiring is simply absent.
- **VERDICT — agreement `UNVERIFIED` · liveness `FAIL` · visibility `NONE` · load-bearing `high`**
  - agreement: AGREEMENT IS N/A — there is no hop, because the endpoint has no producer. I confirm the inventory's finding by independent grep: `grep -rn deliberation repos/*/src scripts/ packages/` returns ONLY the route itself (execution-traces.ts:4243-4295, comments and the handler), three unrelated prose mentions in obsidian-vessel (goal-dispatch-view.ts:2159, :2903; panel-narrative.ts:501) and two in operator-goal-generator. NOTHING POSTs to /deliberation. The producer side is confirmed as filed: recordStep at index.ts:8354-8357, called at seven sites (:8717, :9029, :9300, :9400, :10034, :10068, :10146), pushes into a local walkSteps array and mirrors onto the in-memory executionStore record. I verified the only durability: persistDispatchStore at index.ts:15517-15522 (`Bun.write(DISPATCH_STORE_PATH, JSON.stringify([...executionStore.values()]))`) on `setInterval(..., 5000)` at :15523, with pruneStore at :15524-15529 capping at 100 records and dropping the oldest 20. The two value-forms are compatible (camelCase WalkStep objects vs the route's `body.steps[]` spread into impulse_resolutions); the wiring is simply absent. I mark agreement UNVERIFIED rather than PASS/FAIL because the rubric's binary does not apply to a seam with one end missing — calling it PASS would imply data crosses.
  - **probe:** DEAD BY CONSTRUCTION — settled statically, no probe needed for the hop itself. Confirming probe on a local stack: `SELECT count() FROM execution WHERE impulse_resolutions[WHERE deliberation = true] AND executed_at > time::now() - 24h;` — HEALTHY would be > 0 after any dispatch; EXPECTED = 0, now and forever, until a producer exists. Complementary probe proving the data exists on the other side: dispatch a goal, then `mcp__metabob__resolve_impulse({shape:'goalWalkState', pointer:{dispatchId:'<id>'}})` returns populated steps — showing the record is produced and simply never shipped.
  - visibility: Nothing reports that an endpoint has no callers. The consequence is the serious part: the walk's candidate set, its Thompson draws, its per-candidate rejectedBecause reasons and its per-step pool deltas — the ENTIRE record of HOW selection happened — exist only in a 100-record in-memory ring (pruneStore :15524-15529) mirrored every 5s to a JSON file that is not the trace store, and are lost on restart. This is also what makes R3-reach-14 unfalsifiable after the fact: the walkLog the hollow cap reasons over is never persisted anywhere a later reader can compare it against the verdict it produced.
  - **what must happen:** Wire the producer — the consumer already exists and is correct. (1) FILE repos/goal-host-vessel/src/index.ts, at the end of the walk (beside the deliverReachVerdict call at :14649): POST the accumulated walkSteps to `${ACTIVITY_API_ENDPOINT}/v2/activities/execution-traces/deliberation` with `{execution_id, steps}`, using the same retry-and-report discipline deliverReachVerdict already implements at :945-997 (which is the best-reporting code in this stretch — copy it, do not reinvent it). The route caps at 60 steps and spreads them verbatim, so no format work is needed. (2) COUNTER: `SELECT count() FROM execution WHERE impulse_resolutions[WHERE deliberation = true]` over the last hour, on activity-api /health — HEALTHY > 0. (3) DETECTOR for the CLASS (law 6): a test that enumerates every registered route in activity-api and asserts each has at least one caller in the super-repo — the same corpus-scan technique trace-key-agreement.test.ts uses for keys, applied to endpoints, and scoped to the whole repo rather than one vessel's src. An endpoint with no producer is the coarsest possible dead seam and nothing currently detects it. (4) This is a prerequisite for auditing R3-reach-14 at all.

**Stretch summary.** All 16 seams exist and the producer/consumer attributions are correct — no seam was misidentified. AGREEMENT settled statically on all 16: 5 PASS, 10 FAIL, 1 N/A (an endpoint with no producer). Corrections to the inventory: (1) R3-execute-05's blast radius, filed UNVERIFIED, is now SETTLED and the inventory's implied severity is too high — the three templates using a bare `{{priorTaskId}}` all run on the dev-vessel CLI path, which propagates correctly (cli.ts:168), so there is no demonstrated live outage; the FAIL is a genuine engine-vs-CLI divergence that re-opens the exact dual-convention gap engine.ts:1099-1103 says the line exists to close. Downgraded critical→high. (2) R3-trace-13's "98 of 98" citation is execution-traces.ts:257-261, not :227-236. (3) R3-execute-02's colon-shape claim is stronger than filed: colon-bearing shapes are LIVE pool members (obsidian:write_note, obsidian:note at index.ts:10060, :6819) and a THIRD interpolator the inventory never named — goal-host's own interpolateProxyValue at index.ts:12927 — carries the identical `[\w]+` regex. (4) R3-execute-07's producedBy-predicate FAIL is LATENT: no template in the corpus uses the `{shape, producedBy}` InputShapeRef form, so I mark it PASS with the hazard recorded. Two net-new findings beyond the inventory: horizontal fan-out runs Promise.all branches against ONE shared impulse store where each branch with an undefined bundleParentExecId satisfies isTopLevel and calls store.impulses.clear(), wiping siblings' outputs mid-bundle (index.ts:8960-9007 × engine.ts:1484-1487); and goal-host index.ts:12843 states verbatim the contract engine.ts:1104 broke ("engine sets variables[<taskId>] = that task's first output content") — the crispest available evidence for that regression. The loudest NEGATIVE: trace-key-agreement.test.ts:67 declares consumed_from_task_ids a KNOWN_ORPHAN and asserts it has no reader, while composition-edge-reconcile.ts:342 reads it — the test's corpus (new URL('../') = activity-api/src only) is structurally blind to scripts/, so it passes today, misreports a live seam as dead, and would keep passing after any out-of-tree reader was wired.


## LEARNING 1 — trace ingest to moved posterior (written trace → deriveSignatureShapes/tier fallbacks → signature stamping → applyOutcomeToPosteriors → propagateCreditAlongChain → thompson / context_thompson_scores writes → abstentions)


> HEADLINE ANSWER — does the credit WRITE key coincide with the selection READ key? Three INDEPENDENT decouplings; do not collapse them.

(1) STRUCTURAL. The walk's PRIMARY selection path, POST /v2/activities/discover-by-shapes, never reads context_thompson_scores at all — `grep -n context_thompson_scores repos/activity-api/src/services/discover-by-shapes.ts` returns nothing on HEAD. The conditional posterior can only influence POST /recommend, which goal-host reaches ONLY when discover returned zero candidates (index.ts:8768-8772), on the prefer-composition probe (:8473-8487), and in the single-template recovery loop (:5636). CONFIRMED static.

(2) template_id VALUE FORM (bare write vs prefixed read) — this is R2-select-11's open item. Cross-referenced, deliberately not re-asserted; it needs the same data sample R2 named.

(3) SIGNATURE VALUE FORM — this stretch's contribution. Both sides call the same helper (utils/session-context.ts:154-186, sha256 → 8 bytes → 16 hex), but assemble different pre-images. WRITE: sorted(deriveSignatureShapes(trace)) + provenance from body.metadata.provenance + sorted(body.metadata.missing_shapes). READ: sorted(impulse_shapes ∪ semantics.impliedShapes) + provenance only when impulse_state_space was sent (goal-host never sends it) + `missing: []` HARDCODED (activities.ts:6188). Can they ever be the same string? YES in principle — nothing structural forbids it, and the coincidence regime is pathway replay: an identical pool at the same walk position with an empty missing-set on the write side. Within one walk it is impossible once anything has been produced (goal-host's producedShapes grows monotonically, so the sorted-comma-join differs). DEPENDS ON DATA across walks. MEASUREMENT: on a local stack, emit `stateSpaceSig` and `sigScoresMap.size` at INFO per /recommend, and join that bucket set against `SELECT DISTINCT context_bucket FROM context_thompson_scores WHERE signature_version = 1`. Healthy = a non-zero count of /recommend calls that match a stored bucket for a candidate with n_observations ≥ 5 and ≥2 candidates. Note that even a perfect key match is inert below that floor (activities.ts:6543-6551).

TIER-3 DOMINANCE (the brief's specific ask). deriveSignatureShapes (execution-traces.ts:133-167) advertises three tiers. Tier 2 is STRUCTURALLY DEAD: it reads `t.input_impulse_shapes` / `t.inputShapes` (:151-154) while normalizePersistedTask emits `input_shapes` (:248-249) — the key sets never intersect, and the same is true of tier 3's per-task half (:163-164 vs :250-251). So tier 3 survives only through the two TRACE-level sources, `trace.output_impulse_shapes` and `trace.output_impulses[].shape`. Population split, CONFIRMED static: ias-executor populates tier 1 (trace-sink :198-201 from engine.ts:176) — but as a UNION of the decision-time pool with every task's DECLARED input shapes, which is not the set the read side builds; light-dispatch sends no input_impulse_shapes at all (index.ts:859-895) and so lands tier 3 on 100% of its traces. A tier-3 key is a PRODUCED-pool key being read against an INPUT-pool derivation — for the same activity's re-selection it can never self-match. MEASUREMENT of dominance: `SELECT count() FROM execution WHERE (input_impulse_shapes = NONE OR array::len(input_impulse_shapes) = 0) AND output_impulse_shapes != NONE GROUP ALL` versus total, split by whether tags contain 'dispatcher_used:light-dispatch'. The ingest comment at execution-traces.ts:1911-1917 puts light-dispatch at ~10,166 root executions per 72h, which is the order of magnitude to expect.

e19997f STATUS. `ancestor_signatures` (posterior-update.ts:169-174) has NO populating caller — applyOutcomeToPosteriors builds ExecutionForChainCredit at :1127-1133 without it, and nothing else calls propagateCreditAlongChain in src/. So the trace-row fallback landed by e19997f (`meta?.signature` at :706, from `SELECT ... signature ... FROM v_paradigm_execution_traces`) is the ONLY live source of an ancestor signature. It works — the view does project the column (sql/migrations/167:49-50) — and it writes the ancestor's OWN HISTORICAL pool signature, which is precisely the string the current-pool read cannot reproduce (seam L1-credit-11).

SIGNATURE NAMESPACES. Four coexist in context_thompson_scores: signature_version 1 (the live conditional posterior), signature_version 2 (the repair path written at execution-traces.ts:2147-2158, read only when the tuning row REPAIR_SIGNATURE_CONSUME ≥ 1 — default 0, activities.ts:6212), absent/v0 (the context_bucket path, which has no fleet producer — L1-credit-12), and `cluster:<id>` buckets from applyClusterPosterior. Separately, `repair_signature` ('1f', execution-traces.ts:2165-2177) is stamped on failed traces and its own comment at :2141 says nothing consumes it.

METHOD NOTE ON PROBES. Every probe above is written as a table count or an INFO-level journal line. The salvage file's L3 validator established that LOG_LEVEL defaults to info (config.ts:237) with zero DEBUG lines in 24h, so a probe that greps `posterior_update_v1_conditional` (posterior-update.ts:1049), `chain_credit_no_sig` (:731), `posterior_update` (:217) or `[learning] context_thompson_scores updated` (execution-traces.ts:3142) measures the FILTER, not the seam. The INFO/WARN lines that ARE usable in this stretch: `posterior delta DROPPED — no variant_performance_metrics row` (posterior-aggregator.ts:230), `[reach-patch] late reach verdict graded into posteriors` / `... skipped` (execution-traces.ts:4447,4453), `[learning] activity_template counters updated` (:2849), `POST /recommend` (activities.ts:5869).

CROSS-REFERENCES (not duplicated here): X-boundary-01 owns the trace flush/spool/4xx-unlink hop upstream of L1-credit-01. R2-select-11 owns the template_id bare-vs-prefixed question. R2-select-12 owns the 8-hex `state_signature` rejection by /recommend's 16-hex regex. R2-select-05 owns the discover-side VPM read; L1-credit-09 is its write-side complement. L3-sf-write-04 / L3-psi-10 own the successor-features ψ cell, which rides the SAME v1Sig this stretch produces (execution-traces.ts:3026-3054) and therefore inherits every signature finding above.

WHAT I DID NOT SETTLE (say UNVERIFIED, not zero): the stored form of context_thompson_scores.template_id; the stored form of execution.org_id (bare 'public' vs 'organizations:public') which decides L1-credit-14's view lookup; the actual coincidence rate of write/read signatures; and every liveness verdict — no live access was used, per the access rules, and no credential was read or reconstructed.


### `L1-credit-01` — ias-executor trace → POST /v2/activities/execution-traces: the wire projection that builds input_impulse_shapes

- **Datum:** The whole trace body; specifically top-level `input_impulse_shapes: string[]` — the shape set the v1 state-space signature is derived from.
- **Producer:** repos/ias-executor-ts/src/adapters/activity-api-trace-sink.ts:87-215 — explicit key-by-key body literal. Fires on every completed/failed ExecutionTrace (postWithRetry :226; spool/drain on failure is X-boundary-01, not re-owned here). :198-201 builds `input_impulse_shapes = [...new Set([...trace.inputShapes, ...trace.tasks.flatMap(t => t.inputShapes)])]`. `trace.inputShapes` is set by repos/ias-executor-ts/src/engine.ts:176 = shapes of the SEEDED impulse pool (the decision-time pool), attached at :1306 (completed) and :1425 (failed). Per-task `inputShapes` are the template's DECLARED input shapes (engine.ts:513,530,1272).
- **Consumer:** repos/activity-api/src/routes/execution-traces.ts:1961-1963 — `...(body.input_impulse_shapes && body.input_impulse_shapes.length > 0 ? { input_impulse_shapes: body.input_impulse_shapes } : {})`. Condition: array present AND non-empty; an empty array (no seeded impulses and no declared task inputs) makes the field ABSENT on the trace object, which drops signature derivation to tier 3. Then read at :147 by deriveSignatureShapes (tier 1) and at :3403 by the shape-score path.
- **Value form:** WRITE: deduped union of (pool shapes ∪ declared per-task input shapes), unsorted array of bare shape names. READ at the signature: `dedupe(collect(trace.input_impulse_shapes))` then sorted+comma-joined inside computeStateSpaceSignature (utils/session-context.ts:156,184). The sink's own comment at :191-197 asserts "the write key matches the read key" — that claim is falsified by seam L1-credit-11: the read side unions impliedShapes instead of declared task inputs.
- **VERDICT — agreement `PASS` · liveness `UNOBSERVABLE-PENDING-LOCAL-STACK` · visibility `NONE` · load-bearing `medium`**
  - agreement: Producer/consumer correctly identified and the field name/type agree. activity-api-trace-sink.ts:198-201 builds input_impulse_shapes as a deduped union of trace.inputShapes (engine.ts:176 = the seeded/decision-time pool, attached at :1306 and :1425) with every task's DECLARED inputShapes (engine.ts:513,530,1272); execution-traces.ts:1961-1963 stores it only when present AND non-empty, so an empty array is silently absent and demotes the trace to tier 3. CONFIRMED FALSE DOC-CLAIM: the sink's own comment at :191-197 asserts 'the write key matches the read key' with the /recommend read side — it does not. The read side (activities.ts:5897) unions impulse_shapes with semantics.impliedShapes, never with declared task inputs, so the two pre-images differ whenever any task declares an input shape the pool did not carry. Adversarially: this seam is real and correctly attributed, but its practical weight is low because the id-form failure (L1-credit-10) makes the resulting key unreadable anyway.

HEALTHY = the non-light-dispatch share is ≈100% (every ias-executor trace carries the field); the ingest comment's '~4%' is the unhealthy baseline.
  - visibility: No counter anywhere distinguishes 'poster sent an empty array' from 'poster never sent the field'. Both collapse to an absent column and a tier-3 stamp.
  - **what must happen:** repos/ias-executor-ts/src/adapters/activity-api-trace-sink.ts:198 — emit the field unconditionally and add a sibling counter, or (better) stop unioning declared task inputs so the write pre-image equals the read pre-image (pool shapes only). DETECTOR: an activity-api ingest counter `trace_input_shapes_empty_total{poster}` incremented at execution-traces.ts:1961 when the array is present-but-empty, so 'sent nothing' and 'sent an empty pool' stop looking identical.

### `L1-credit-05` — learning_track='system' early return — the whole posterior/psi/edge cluster abstains before any of it runs

- **Datum:** The abstention decision itself: which traces are eligible for ANY learning write.
- **Producer:** repos/activity-api/src/routes/execution-traces.ts:2072-2077 — `learningTrack = await resolveLearningTrack(trace.activity_id)`; any throw is caught and falls through to 'unclassified'. The cached `learning_track` field on the activity template is the datum.
